Expand description

A Pantone Fashion, Home + Interiors (FHI) color.

The enum covers the 2310 colors of the Pantone FHI system - each color is identified by a NN-NNNN code and a name (Pantone 19-4057 TCX True Blue). In contrast to crate::PantoneNamedColor (the print-mixing PMS vocabulary), FHI colors describe dyed or coated material swatches and share their names across the substrate suffixes - see PantoneFhiSuffix. The canonical string uses TCX; TPX is discontinued and TPG is the current paper guide.

FromStr accepts the canonical Pantone <code> TCX <name> form, a bare <code> or <code> <suffix> (19-4057, 19-4057 TPX) and the bare color name (True Blue), all case-insensitively. Display and the wire format use the canonical form.

The stored sRGB values are community approximations of the physical swatches as published in the pantone-colors dataset; FHI colors are defined for textiles and interiors, so the values are indicative rather than authoritative. PANTONE is a trademark of Pantone LLC.

Implementations§

Source§

impl PantoneFhiNamedColor

Source

pub const ALL: &'static [Self]

All FHI colors, sorted by code.

Source

pub const fn code(&self) -> &'static str

The NN-NNNN color code ("19-4057").

Source

pub const fn name(&self) -> &'static str

The color name without code and suffix ("True Blue").

Source

pub const fn suffix(&self) -> PantoneFhiSuffix

The canonical substrate suffix used by Self::as_str.

FHI colors are material-independent names; the canonical string resolves the cotton TCX guide, which is the dataset these sRGB approximations are based on.

Source

pub const fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str

The canonical Pantone <code> TCX <name> string.

Source

pub const fn to_color(&self) -> Color<u8>

The color as opaque byte components.

Source

pub fn nearest(color: &Color<u8>) -> Self

The nearest FHI color to color in the sRGB space - squared Euclidean distance over the RGB channels, alpha ignored.
